- **phylogenetic-compositional-pca-analysis**: Use when you have a paired metagenomics feature table (e.g. n=666 fecal SPF samples) and sample metadata, and need to test whether community composition differs by a factor such as genotype. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/phylogenetic-compositional-pca-analysis))
- **phylogenetic-distance-computation**: Use when you have 16S OTUs (e.g. HMP V1V3/V3V5 clustered at 97% PSD with QIIME) and need to quantify how compact each OTU is under MSD and BLD rather than the clustering threshold metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-distance-computation))
- **phylogenetic-distance-transformation**: Use when you have a cophenetic distance matrix (from ape's cophenetic function) over microbial features and need phylogeny-aware feature ordering/clustering before model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-distance-transformation))
- **phylogenetic-distance-weighting**: Use when 16S marker gene OTUs must be mapped to predicted gene families (KO/COG) for organisms without reference genomes, and a phylogenetic reference tree with annotated tips (e.g. Greengenes + IMG) is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-distance-weighting))
- **phylogenetic-diversity-calculation**: Use when you have an OTU/species count table per sample plus a phylogenetic tree and need per-sample phylogenetic α-diversity indices (PD, PE, PQE, or richness/Shannon/Simpson) as regression outcomes for downstream meta-analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-diversity-calculation))
- **phylogenetic-gcn-correction**: Use when 16S rRNA amplicon count tables (biom, QIIME, generic) need GCN-bias correction, or when qPCR-derived total abundance must be adjusted by average community GCN before downstream diversity or enterotype analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-gcn-correction))
- **phylogenetic-independent-contrasts-ancestral-state-estimation**: Use when Greengenes OTUs or taxa lack sequenced genomes (hence no measured 16S rRNA copy number) and a phylogenetically-based GCN estimate is needed for CopyRighter correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-independent-contrasts-ancestral-state-estimation))
- **phylogenetic-inference**: Use when building a maximum-likelihood tree from DNA, codon, or protein sequences of any organism: alignment, trimming, model choice, support, rooting, reporting. Not for gene-tree discordance or selection tests (species-tree-and-selection). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/phylogenetic-inference))
- **phylogenetic-marker-benchmarking**: Use when assessing whether DIAMOND's tantan masking suppresses alignments during Patchwork marker retrieval — especially for read-based query data — by A/B comparing --masking 0 against the default masking configuration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-marker-benchmarking))
- **phylogenetic-methods**: Comprehensive guide to phylogenetic tree building methods including distance-based (UPGMA, Neighbor-Joining), maximum likelihood (RAxML, IQ-TREE), and Bayesian inference (MrBayes). Covers multiple sequence alignment, distance matrices, bootstrap analysis, consensus methods, tree formats (Newick, Nexus), and tree comparison metrics. Includes implementation patterns for tree visualization and evaluation. ([roeimed0/rrna-phylo](https://github.com/roeimed0/rrna-phylo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/phylogenetic-methods))
- **phylogenetic-node-completeness-scoring**: Use when OrthoFinder rooted gene trees and an of2orthomap-derived orthomap (with LCA gene age classes) are available for a focal species such as Danio rerio, and you need per-orthogroup completeness along the lineage to flag low-continuity, HGT-suspect orthogroups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-node-completeness-scoring))
- **phylogenetic-operad-acset**: Baez-Otter phylogenetic operad Phyl = Com + [0,∞) as ACSet schema, with mathpix-gem extraction and ∞-operad dendroidal integration. ([plurigrid/asi](https://github.com/plurigrid/asi/tree/HEAD/skills/phylogenetic-operad-acset) · ★72)
- **phylogenetic-placement-of-16s-amplicon-reads**: Use when 16S amplicon reads from pre-clustered OTUs (e.g., HMP V1V3/V3V5 at a 97% similarity threshold) must be placed onto a fixed reference tree to compute MSD and BLD between a representative sequence and all remaining OTU members. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-placement-of-16s-amplicon-reads))
- **phylogenetic-sequence-placement**: Use when individual short 16S reads or OTU member sequences must be assigned lineages via phylogenetic placement on a reference tree, or when BLD between representative and member sequences must be computed for HMP-style V1V3/V3V5 OTUs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-sequence-placement))
- **phylogenetic-signal-estimation**: Use when you need to test whether a quantitative trait estimated for OTUs or taxa (e.g., 16S rRNA GCN from CopyRighter's Greengenes data files) shows significant phylogenetic autocorrelation on a tree. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-signal-estimation))
- **phylogenetic-style-dendrogram-visualization**: Use when a correlation matrix (Pearson, Spearman, or MIC) has been converted to correlation distance d(x,y)=1-|corr(x,y)| and you must visualize the hierarchy of merged clusters as a color-coded dendrogram in one of the AMADA styles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-style-dendrogram-visualization))
- **phylogenetic-substitution-model-selection**: Use when a cleaned multiple alignment of consensus genomes plus database sequences must be converted into phylogenetic trees under both maximum-likelihood and Bayesian inference, and a substitution model compatible with both programs is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-substitution-model-selection))
- **phylogenetic-subtree-entropy-aggregation**: Use when you need to determine at which taxonomic or phylogenetic resolution environment (habitat) specificity is captured in a large multi-study 16S dataset such as EMP 16S Release 1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-subtree-entropy-aggregation))
- **phylogenetic-taxon-read-counting**: Use when you have small RNA-seq reads that failed to map to the human genome (e.g. STAR *_Aligned_UnMapped.bam) and need microbial species-level read counts per taxon. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-taxon-read-counting))
- **phylogenetic-tree-based-population-modeling**: Use when simulating single-cell RNA-seq data for multiple discrete populations or continuous trajectories whose relationships should follow a lineage/phylogenetic tree, and you need to validate that the simulated heterogeneity (Sigma) preserves tree structure in low-dimensional embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-based-population-modeling))
- **phylogenetic-tree-breadth-first-clustering**: Use when you have a single-cell lineage tree (Newick/phylo with cells as leaves) and need to partition tip cells into a fixed number of subpopulations that each correspond to a clade of the reconstructed phylogeny. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-breadth-first-clustering))
- **phylogenetic-tree-construction-from-otu-sequences**: Use when a filtered, dereplicated, chimera-checked set of representative OTU sequences (e.g. result/otus.fa) must be converted into a phylogenetic tree to enable phylogenetically aware beta diversity (UniFrac) and PCoA ordination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-construction-from-otu-sequences))
- **phylogenetic-tree-feature-metadata-mapping**: Use when a Qemistree chemical hierarchy (e.g., qemistree.qza from make-hierarchy) exists for an untargeted LC-MS/MS feature table and you need chemical-class labels on tree tips, or class-level subtree patterns interpreted against sample metadata such as food origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-feature-metadata-mapping))
- **phylogenetic-tree-inference**: Use when starting a PM-CNN analysis from an OTU/species feature table whose representative nucleotide sequences are available (e.g. from GreenGenes) and no phylogenetic tree in NEWICK format has yet been supplied. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-inference--810b24b4baa5af61))
- **phylogenetic-tree-inference**: Use when you have a FASTA of de-novo clustered OTU representative sequences (e.g. from micca otu) and need a Newick phylogenetic tree with one leaf per OTU for downstream diversity or ecology analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-inference--9794c627cabda570))
- **phylogenetic-tree-inference**: Use when you have amino acid sequences of a protein family (e.g., FASTA of homologs across many genomes) and need an evolutionary tree relating them, starting from an unaligned FASTA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-inference))
- **phylogenetic-tree-manipulation-in-r**: Use when a reconstructed lineage or phylogenetic tree (e.g., Newick format from LinRace) must be partitioned at a specified depth and tip-level annotations (e.g., 34 cell types over 9707 mouse embryo cells) summarized per subtree in R. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-manipulation-in-r))
- **phylogenetic-tree-parsing-newick**: Use when a phylogenetic tree exists in NEWICK format (from FastTree/Mafft or user-provided) and a pairwise tip-to-tip distance matrix is needed to encode phylogenetic relationships between OTUs/species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-parsing-newick))
- **phylogenetic-tree-reconstruction-from-spectral-distances**: Use when a lower-triangular pairwise distance matrix over tandem mass spectrometry datasets (e.g., MGF files) is available and a rooted tree in Newick format is needed for molecular phylogenetics or sample relatedness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-reconstruction-from-spectral-distances))
- **phylogenetic-tree-rendering-and-polyphyletic-cluster-detection**: Use when a population-genomics pipeline has produced IBS or FST distance matrices and TreeMix tree files, and you need interactive visual trees plus detection of populations that split across clades (polyphyly). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-rendering-and-polyphyletic-cluster-detection))
- **phylogenetic-tree-structured-compositional-modeling**: Use when the input is compositional taxa/count abundance data (e.g., microbiome or diet-taxa tables) with a known phylogenetic tree, and the goal is to identify covariate-branch associations while respecting tree-like structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-structured-compositional-modeling))
- **phylogenetic-tree-styler**: Analyze data with `phylogenetic-tree-styler` using a reproducible workflow, explicit validation, and structured outputs for review-ready interpretation. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Data%20Analysis/phylogenetic-tree-styler) · ★2.1k)
- **phylogenetic-tree-topology-comparison**: Use when several lineage or phylogenetic trees have been reconstructed from the same cell set (e.g., competing lineage-reconstruction methods) and you need to compare their local resolution via maximum root-to-tip depth and internal-node counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-topology-comparison))
- **phylogenetic-tree-traversal-and-subtree-analysis**: Use when a rooted phylogeny with cells as leaves must be traversed subtree-by-subtree (e.g., to compute per-cell plasticity of categorical meta-data such as GEO GSM4905334 annotations), or when per-leaf summaries require averaging node-level statistics along root-to-leaf paths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-traversal-and-subtree-analysis))
- **phylogenetic-tree-validation**: Use when after generating a Chemical Feature Tree from q2-qemistree (or any tree artifact) and before using it for alpha-diversity or beta-diversity phylogenetic analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-validation) · 3 copies)
- **phylogenetic-tree-visualization**: Use when you need to visualize a large phylogeny (up to thousands of taxa, e.g., a 3,737-taxon microbial tree of life) together with genome- or clade-level metadata such as KEGG module presence or genome size in a single compact figure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-visualization))
- **phylogenetic-tree-visualization**: Use when a single-cell lineage-tracing dataset has a reconstructed phylogeny (e.g. CRISPR-Cas9 lineage trees from Cassiopeia) that must be interactively browsed, selected, and overlaid with UMAP/tSNE projections and metadata in a shareable web report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-visualization))
- **phylogenetic-tree-visualization-ete3**: Use when running or reproducing the scalepopgen TreeMix phylogeny sub-workflow and you need to plot the inferred population trees, bootstrap-supported consensus trees, or migration-edge results from filtered biallelic SNP data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-tree-visualization-ete3))
- **phylogenetic-vs-taxonomic-abundance-correction**: Use when 16S rRNA amplicon abundance profiles need GCN bias correction and a decision must be made between phylogenetic-level and taxonomic-level CopyRighter correction, or when validating corrected profiles against known community compositions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/phylogenetic-vs-taxonomic-abundance-correction))
